- Static source inspection only. No producer invocation, plan observation, apply, tests, target compilation, runtime verification or final approval was performed.
- Catalog aggregate-repository-port overstates this profile: repositoryTemplate emits a concrete local actor cache, not a separate repository interface/infra implementation pair.
- Catalog route-definition should bind embedded service-runtime Loader router, not assume service-config conf/routes emission.
- PageRenderer/Authority/Factory are interfaces and pins; external application markup, auth implementation and composition factory remain handwritten/external.
- service.projections is explicitly rejected; journal projectionStore support is a distinct implemented branch, not generic JDBC projection generation.
- Many catalog refs are generated Scala names rather than JS exports: ApplicationBootstrap, PageRenderer, externalSourceRoots and service-unit.conf must map to actual renderer exports/file locators.
- Catalog event-handler must map renderInvariant, external-event-bridge renderCommand, value-object renderValues, exact-value-codec renderConverters, protobuf-serializer renderAdapters; renderAggregate/typeName are not their final renderers.
- serialization-adapter, converter, rules, aggregate, build and service-runtime kinds multiplex distinct output shapes; map category by renderer+shape, not alias membership.
- Runtime engines/coordinators are consumed as external libraries; generation does not implement Monarch or strict Saga engine internals.
- Generated test coverage is explicitly fixture-specific: finite host cases target context inventory; execution/effect test renderers target allocation-inspection (effect step inspect). Nonmatching business inputs may produce no such tests or an empty host suite; generated-test kind is not general business coverage.

### service-dsl

- Manifest：`generators/service-dsl/generator.json`；版本 0.1.0；legacy/legacy。
- 输入：ServiceSpec: kind application(default)|query|domain|port, name/package and method/port/dependency descriptions; port optionally supplies bridge.fq/target.
- 生产链：entrypoint=`js:generators/service-dsl/emit.mjs#*`；entrypoint=`js:generators/service-dsl/parse.mjs#*`；input=`js:generators/service-dsl/parse.mjs#*`；input=`js:generators/service-dsl/emit.mjs#*`；implementation=`js:generators/service-dsl/parse.mjs#*`；implementation=`js:generators/service-dsl/emit.mjs#*`；writer=`js:generators/service-dsl/emit.mjs#*`
